
📁 admin\about\about.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import styles from "./about.module.css";

import { getMyInstitute } from "../../../../api/instituteApi";
import { getDepartments } from "../../../../api/departmentApi";
import { getCourses } from "../../../../api/courseApi";
import { getSubjects } from "../../../../api/subjectApi";

function About() {
  const [institute, setInstitute] = useState(null);
  const [departments, setDepartments] = useState([]);
  const [coursesMap, setCoursesMap] = useState({});
  const [subjectsMap, setSubjectsMap] = useState({});

  /* ================= FETCH DATA ================= */
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAll = async () => {
      try {
        const inst = await getMyInstitute();
        setInstitute(inst);

        // 🔥 TYPE CHECK
        if (inst?.type === "college") {
          const depts = await getDepartments();
          setDepartments(depts);

          const coursesTemp = {};
          const subjectsTemp = {};

          for (let dept of depts) {
            const courses = await getCourses(dept._id);
            const subjects = await getSubjects(dept._id);

            coursesTemp[dept._id] = courses;
            subjectsTemp[dept._id] = subjects;
          }

          setCoursesMap(coursesTemp);
          setSubjectsMap(subjectsTemp);
        }

      } catch (err) {
        console.error("ERROR:", err);
      }
    };

    fetchAll();
  }, []);

  if (!institute) return <p>Loading...</p>;

  const { name, address, logo, buildingImage, type } = institute;

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>

      {/* ================= HERO ================= */}
      <div
        className={styles.hero}
        style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${buildingImage})` }}
      >
        <div className={styles.overlay}>
          <img src={logo} alt="logo" className={styles.logo} />
          <h1>{name}</h1>
          <p>{address}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.content}>
        <h2 className={styles.heading}>Academic Structure</h2>

        {/* ===================================================== */}
        {/* ================= COLLEGE VIEW ======================= */}
        {/* ===================================================== */}
        {type === "college" &&
          departments.map((dept) => (
            <div key={dept._id} className={styles.card}>

              {/* DEPARTMENT */}
              <div className={styles.cardHeader}>
                <div>
                  <h3>{dept.name}</h3>
                  <p className={styles.desc}>
                    {dept.about || "No description"}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <span className={styles.hod}>
                  HOD: {dept.hod?.name || "N/A"}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* COURSES */}
              {(coursesMap[dept._id] || []).map((course) => (
                <div key={course._id} className={styles.course}>

                  <div className={styles.courseHeader}>
                    <div>
                      <h4>{course.name}</h4>

                      <p className={styles.desc}>
                        {course.details?.description || "No description"}
                      </p>

                      <div className={styles.metaLine}>
                        <b>Duration:</b> {course.duration} yrs
                      </div>

                      <div className={styles.metaLine}>
                        <b>Type:</b> {course.type}
                      </div>

                      <div className={styles.metaLine}>
                        <b>Eligibility:</b>{" "}
                        {course.details?.eligibility || "N/A"}
                      </div>

                      <div className={styles.metaLine}>
                        <b>Admission:</b>{" "}
                        {course.details?.admission || "N/A"}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* SUBJECTS (⚠️ currently dept-based) */}
                  <div className={styles.semGrid}>
                    {(subjectsMap[dept._id] || []).map((sub) => (
                      <div key={sub._id} className={styles.semCard}>
                        <h5>{sub.name}</h5>
                        <p className={styles.meta}>
                          Code: {sub.code || "N/A"}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ))}

        {/* ===================================================== */}
        {/* ================= SCHOOL VIEW ======================== */}
        {/* ===================================================== */}
        {type === "school" && (
          <div className={styles.card}>
            <h3>School Structure</h3>

            <p className={styles.desc}>
              Classes, sections, and subjects are managed via configuration.
            </p>

            {/* ⚠️ Since school structure is only in config API */}
            <p style={{ marginTop: "10px" }}>
              Please integrate <b>getInstituteConfig()</b> to render:
              <br />
              • Classes  
              • Sections  
              • Streams  
              • Subjects  
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default About;

📁 admin\aboutInstitute\aboutInstitute.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import SchoolSetup from "./schoolSetup";
import CollegeSetup from "./collegeSetup";
import ImageUploader from "./ImageUploader";
import About from "./../about/about";

import {
  getMyInstitute,
  setAboutInstitute,
} from "../../../../api/instituteApi";

import styles from "./AboutInstitute.module.css";

function AboutInstitute() {
  const [type, setType] = useState("");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [about, setAbout] = useState("");

  const [logo, setLogo] = useState(null);
  const [building, setBuilding] = useState(null);
  const [gallery, setGallery] = useState([]);

  // config removed from backend usage
  const [config, setConfig] = useState({});

  const [data, setData] = useState(null);
  const [editMode, setEditMode] = useState(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  /* ================= FETCH ================= */
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchData = async () => {
      try {
        const d = await getMyInstitute(); // already res.data

        if (d) {
          setData(d);

          // PREFILL
          setName(d.name || "");
          setAbout(d.address || ""); // backend uses address
          setType(d.type || "");
          setLogo(d.logo || null);
          setBuilding(d.buildingImage || null);
          setGallery(d.gallery || []);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log("No institute found");
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    fetchData();
  }, []);

  /* ================= VALIDATION ================= */
  const validate = () => {
    if (!name.trim()) return "Institute name required";
    if (!type) return "Select institute type";
    return null;
  };

  /* ================= SUBMIT ================= */
  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    const error = validate();
    if (error) {
      alert(error);
      return;
    }

    try {
      // ONLY UPDATE API EXISTS NOW
      const res = await setAboutInstitute({
        name,
        about, // maps to address internally
        type,
        logo,
        buildingImage: building,
        gallery,
      });

      alert("Saved ✅");

      const updated = res;

      // SYNC STATE
      setData(updated);
      setEditMode(false);

      setName(updated.name || "");
      setAbout(updated.address || "");
      setType(updated.type || "");
      setLogo(updated.logo || null);
      setBuilding(updated.buildingImage || null);
      setGallery(updated.gallery || []);

    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert("Error saving institute");
    }
  };

  /* ================= LOADING ================= */
  if (loading) {
    return <p style={{ padding: "20px" }}>Loading...</p>;
  }

  /* ================= VIEW MODE ================= */
  if (data && !editMode) {
    return (
      <div>
        <About data={data} />

        <div className={styles.editBtnWrapper}>
          <button
            className={styles.primaryBtn}
            onClick={() => setEditMode(true)}
          >
            Edit Institute
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* ================= FORM MODE ================= */
  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <h2 className={styles.title}>
        {data ? "Edit Institute" : "Setup Your Institute"}
      </h2>

      <input
        placeholder="Institute Name"
        className={styles.name}
        value={name}
        onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
      />

      <textarea
        rows="6"
        placeholder="About your institute"
        className={styles.textarea}
        value={about}
        onChange={(e) => setAbout(e.target.value)}
      />

      {/* ================= IMAGES ================= */}
      <div className={styles.imageUpload}>
        <ImageUploader
          image={logo}
          setImage={setLogo}
          id="logoUpload"
          label="Institute Logo"
        />

        <ImageUploader
          image={building}
          setImage={setBuilding}
          id="buildingUpload"
          label="Building Image"
        />
      </div>

      {/* ================= TYPE ================= */}
      {!type && (
        <div className={styles.selector}>
          <button onClick={() => setType("school")} className={styles.btn}>
            School
          </button>

          <button onClick={() => setType("college")} className={styles.btn}>
            College / University
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ================= SETUP ================= */}
      {type === "school" && (
        <SchoolSetup config={config} setConfig={setConfig} />
      )}

      {type === "college" && (
        <CollegeSetup config={config} setConfig={setConfig} />
      )}

      {/* ================= ACTION ================= */}
      <button className={styles.primaryBtn} onClick={handleSubmit}>
        {data ? "Update Institute" : "Save Institute"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default AboutInstitute;

// export default CollegeSetup;
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import styles from "./collegeSetup.module.css";

import {
  getDepartments,
  createDepartment,
  deleteDepartment,
} from "../../../../api/departmentApi";

import {
  getCourses,
  createCourse,
  deleteCourse,
} from "../../../../api/courseApi";

import {
  getSubjects,
  createSubject,
  deleteSubject,
} from "../../../../api/subjectApi";

import {
  createHod,
  createTeacher,
  createStudent,
  getUsers,
} from "../../../../api/userAPI";

function CollegeSetup() {

  const [departments, setDepartments] = useState([]);
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([]);
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState([]);

  const [selectedDept, setSelectedDept] = useState("");
  const [selectedCourse, setSelectedCourse] = useState("");

  /* ================= INPUTS ================= */

  const [deptName, setDeptName] = useState("");
  const [deptAbout, setDeptAbout] = useState("");

  const [hod, setHod] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    employeeId: "",
    subjectIds: []
  });

  const [teacher, setTeacher] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    employeeId: "",
    subjectIds: []
  });

  const [student, setStudent] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    rollNumber: "",
    courseId: "",
    semester: "",
    departmentId: ""
  });

  const [courseName, setCourseName] = useState("");
  const [subjectName, setSubjectName] = useState("");

  /* ================= FETCH ================= */

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAll();
  }, []);

  const fetchAll = async () => {
    const deptRes = await getDepartments();
    setDepartments(deptRes || []);

    const courseRes = await getCourses();
    setCourses(courseRes || []);

    const subjectRes = await getSubjects();
    setSubjects(subjectRes || []);
  };

  /* ================= ADD ================= */

  const handleAddDepartment = async () => {
    if (!deptName) return;

    await createDepartment({ name: deptName, about: deptAbout });

    setDeptName("");
    setDeptAbout("");
    fetchAll();
  };

  const handleCreateHod = async () => {
    const { name, email, employeeId, subjectIds } = hod;

    if (!name || !email || !employeeId || !selectedDept) {
      alert("Fill all fields");
      return;
    }

    await createHod({
      ...hod,
      departmentId: selectedDept,
    });

    setHod({ name: "", email: "", employeeId: "", subjectIds: [] });
    fetchAll();
  };

  const handleAddTeacher = async () => {
    const { name, email, employeeId, subjectIds } = teacher;

    if (!name || !email || !employeeId || !selectedDept) {
      alert("Fill all fields");
      return;
    }

    await createTeacher({
      ...teacher,
      departmentId: selectedDept,
    });

    setTeacher({ name: "", email: "", employeeId: "", subjectIds: [] });
    fetchAll();
  };

  const handleAddStudent = async () => {
    const { name, email, rollNumber, courseId, semester } = student;

    if (!name || !email || !rollNumber || !courseId || !semester || !selectedDept) {
      alert("Fill all fields");
      return;
    }

    await createStudent({
      ...student,
      departmentId: selectedDept
    });

    setStudent({
      name: "",
      email: "",
      rollNumber: "",
      courseId: "",
      semester: "",
      departmentId: ""
    });
  };

  const handleAddCourse = async () => {
    if (!courseName || !selectedDept) return;

    await createCourse({
      name: courseName,
      departmentId: selectedDept,
    });

    setCourseName("");
    fetchAll();
  };

  const handleAddSubject = async () => {
    if (!subjectName || !selectedDept) return;

    await createSubject({
      name: subjectName,
      departmentId: selectedDept,
    });

    setSubjectName("");
    fetchAll();
  };

  /* ================= UI ================= */

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <h2>College Setup</h2>

      {/* ================= DEPARTMENT ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <input
          placeholder="Department Name"
          value={deptName}
          onChange={(e) => setDeptName(e.target.value)}
        />
        <textarea
          placeholder="About Department"
          value={deptAbout}
          onChange={(e) => setDeptAbout(e.target.value)}
        />
        <button onClick={handleAddDepartment}>Add Department</button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= SELECT DEPT ================= */}
      <select onChange={(e) => setSelectedDept(e.target.value)}>
        <option>Select Department</option>
        {departments.map(d => (
          <option key={d._id} value={d._id}>{d.name}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      {/* ================= HOD ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h3>Create HOD</h3>

        <input placeholder="Name" value={hod.name} onChange={e => setHod({ ...hod, name: e.target.value })} />
        <input placeholder="Email" value={hod.email} onChange={e => setHod({ ...hod, email: e.target.value })} />
        <input placeholder="Employee ID" value={hod.employeeId} onChange={e => setHod({ ...hod, employeeId: e.target.value })} />

        <select multiple onChange={e => {
          const vals = Array.from(e.target.selectedOptions, o => o.value);
          setHod({ ...hod, subjectIds: vals });
        }}>
          {subjects.map(s => (
            <option key={s._id} value={s._id}>{s.name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <button onClick={handleCreateHod}>Create HOD</button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= TEACHER ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h3>Add Teacher</h3>

        <input placeholder="Name" value={teacher.name} onChange={e => setTeacher({ ...teacher, name: e.target.value })} />
        <input placeholder="Email" value={teacher.email} onChange={e => setTeacher({ ...teacher, email: e.target.value })} />
        <input placeholder="Employee ID" value={teacher.employeeId} onChange={e => setTeacher({ ...teacher, employeeId: e.target.value })} />

        <select multiple onChange={e => {
          const vals = Array.from(e.target.selectedOptions, o => o.value);
          setTeacher({ ...teacher, subjectIds: vals });
        }}>
          {subjects.map(s => (
            <option key={s._id} value={s._id}>{s.name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <button onClick={handleAddTeacher}>Add Teacher</button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= STUDENT ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h3>Add Student</h3>

        <input placeholder="Name" value={student.name} onChange={e => setStudent({ ...student, name: e.target.value })} />
        <input placeholder="Email" value={student.email} onChange={e => setStudent({ ...student, email: e.target.value })} />
        <input placeholder="Roll Number" value={student.rollNumber} onChange={e => setStudent({ ...student, rollNumber: e.target.value })} />

        <select onChange={e => setStudent({ ...student, courseId: e.target.value })}>
          <option>Select Course</option>
          {courses.map(c => (
            <option key={c._id} value={c._id}>{c.name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <input
          placeholder="Semester"
          type="number"
          value={student.semester}
          onChange={e => setStudent({ ...student, semester: e.target.value })}
        />

        <button onClick={handleAddStudent}>Add Student</button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= COURSE ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h3>Add Course</h3>
        <input value={courseName} onChange={e => setCourseName(e.target.value)} />
        <button onClick={handleAddCourse}>Add Course</button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= SUBJECT ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h3>Add Subject</h3>
        <input value={subjectName} onChange={e => setSubjectName(e.target.value)} />
        <button onClick={handleAddSubject}>Add Subject</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default CollegeSetup;

// function ImageUploader({ image, setImage, id, label }){
//   const handleChange =async(e) =>{
//     const file = e.target.files[0];

//     if (file && file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
//       setImage(URL.createObjectURL(file));

//       const formData=new FormData();
//       formData.append("image",file);

//   };
import { useState } from "react";
import styles from "./AboutInstitute.module.css";
import API from "./../../../../api/axios";

function ImageUploader({ image, setImage, id, label }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleChange = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];

    // Validate file
    if (!file || !file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
      alert("Please select a valid image file");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);

      // Show preview instantly
      const preview = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      setImage(preview);

      // Upload to backend
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append("image", file);

      const res = await API.post("/upload", formData, {
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data",
        },
      });

      //Replace preview with actual URL
      setImage(res.data.url);

    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert("Image upload failed");
      setImage(null);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const removeImage = () => {
    setImage(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className={styles.uploadBlock}>
      <p className={styles.uploadLabel}>{label}</p>

      <input
        type="file"
        accept="image/*"
        id={id}
        className={styles.hiddenInput}
        onChange={handleChange}
      />

      {!image ? (
        <label htmlFor={id} className={styles.uploadBox}>
          <span className={styles.uploadIcon}>⬆</span>
          <p>Upload</p>
        </label>
      ) : (
        <div className={styles.previewBox}>
          <img src={image} alt="preview" className={styles.previewImage} />

          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.removeBtn}
            onClick={removeImage}
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default ImageUploader;


📁 admin\aboutInstitute\schoolSetup.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import styles from "./schoolSetup.module.css";

/* GLOBAL SUBJECT LIST (MASTER) */
const GLOBAL_SUBJECTS = [
  { _id: "1", name: "Mathematics", code: "MTH101" },
  { _id: "2", name: "Physics", code: "PHY101" },
  { _id: "3", name: "Chemistry", code: "CHM101" },
  { _id: "4", name: "Computer Science", code: "CSE101" },
];

const sectionOptions = ["A", "B", "C"];
const subjectTypes = ["compulsory", "elective", "fun"];

function SchoolSetup({ config = {}, setConfig }) {

  const [classes, setClasses] = useState([]);
  const [classConfig, setClassConfig] = useState({});
  const [schoolType, setSchoolType] = useState("");

  /* ================= TEACHERS ================= */
  const [teachers, setTeachers] = useState([]);
  const [teacherInput, setTeacherInput] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    employeeId: "",
    subjectIds: []
  });

  /* ================= STUDENTS ================= */
  const [studentInput, setStudentInput] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    rollNo: "",
    classId: "",
    section: ""
  });

  /* ================= LOAD ================= */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (config && Object.keys(config).length > 0) {
      setClassConfig(config);
      setClasses(Object.keys(config));
    }
  }, [config]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof setConfig === "function") {
      setConfig(classConfig);
    }
  }, [classConfig]);

  /* ================= GENERATE CLASSES ================= */
  const generateClasses = () => {
    let max = 0;
    if (schoolType === "primary") max = 5;
    else if (schoolType === "secondary") max = 10;
    else if (schoolType === "senior") max = 12;

    const arr = [];
    for (let i = 1; i <= max; i++) arr.push(i.toString());

    setClasses(arr);
  };

  /* ================= UPDATE ================= */
  const updateClass = (cls, updater) => {
    setClassConfig(prev => {
      const base = {
        sections: prev[cls]?.sections || [],
        subjects: prev[cls]?.subjects || [],
        students: prev[cls]?.students || []
      };

      return {
        ...prev,
        [cls]: updater(base)
      };
    });
  };

  const toggle = (val, list = []) =>
    list.includes(val)
      ? list.filter(i => i !== val)
      : [...list, val];

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <h2>School Setup</h2>

      {/* ================= ADD TEACHER ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h3>Add Teacher</h3>

        <input placeholder="Name"
          value={teacherInput.name}
          onChange={e => setTeacherInput({...teacherInput, name: e.target.value})}
        />

        <input placeholder="Email"
          value={teacherInput.email}
          onChange={e => setTeacherInput({...teacherInput, email: e.target.value})}
        />

        <input placeholder="Employee ID"
          value={teacherInput.employeeId}
          onChange={e => setTeacherInput({...teacherInput, employeeId: e.target.value})}
        />

        {/* MULTI SUBJECT SELECT */}
        <select multiple
          value={teacherInput.subjectIds}
          onChange={e => {
            const vals = Array.from(e.target.selectedOptions, o => o.value);
            setTeacherInput({...teacherInput, subjectIds: vals});
          }}
        >
          {GLOBAL_SUBJECTS.map(s => (
            <option key={s._id} value={s._id}>{s.name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <button onClick={() => {
          if (!teacherInput.name) return;

          setTeachers(prev => [...prev, {
            ...teacherInput,
            id: Date.now().toString()
          }]);

          setTeacherInput({
            name: "",
            email: "",
            employeeId: "",
            subjectIds: []
          });
        }}>
          Add Teacher
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= ADD STUDENT ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h3>Add Student</h3>

        <input placeholder="Name"
          value={studentInput.name}
          onChange={e => setStudentInput({...studentInput, name: e.target.value})}
        />

        <input placeholder="Email"
          value={studentInput.email}
          onChange={e => setStudentInput({...studentInput, email: e.target.value})}
        />

        <input placeholder="Roll No"
          value={studentInput.rollNo}
          onChange={e => setStudentInput({...studentInput, rollNo: e.target.value})}
        />

        <select onChange={e => setStudentInput({...studentInput, classId: e.target.value})}>
          <option>Select Class</option>
          {classes.map(c => <option key={c}>{c}</option>)}
        </select>

        <select onChange={e => setStudentInput({...studentInput, section: e.target.value})}>
          <option>Select Section</option>
          {sectionOptions.map(s => <option key={s}>{s}</option>)}
        </select>

        <button onClick={() => {
          const { classId } = studentInput;
          if (!classId) return;

          updateClass(classId, prev => ({
            ...prev,
            students: [...prev.students, studentInput]
          }));

          setStudentInput({
            name: "",
            email: "",
            rollNo: "",
            classId: "",
            section: ""
          });
        }}>
          Add Student
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= CLASS ================= */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <select onChange={e => setSchoolType(e.target.value)}>
          <option>Select Type</option>
          <option value="primary">Primary</option>
          <option value="secondary">Secondary</option>
          <option value="senior">Senior</option>
        </select>

        <button onClick={generateClasses}>Generate Classes</button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= CLASS LOOP ================= */}
      {classes.map(cls => {
        const cfg = classConfig[cls] || {};

        return (
          <div key={cls} className={styles.card}>
            <h3>Class {cls}</h3>

            {/* SECTIONS */}
            {sectionOptions.map(sec => (
              <label key={sec}>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={(cfg.sections || []).includes(sec)}
                  onChange={() =>
                    updateClass(cls, prev => ({
                      ...prev,
                      sections: toggle(sec, prev.sections)
                    }))
                  }
                />
                {sec}
              </label>
            ))}

            {/* SUBJECT UI */}
            <SubjectUI
              stored={cfg.subjects || []}
              teachers={teachers}
              onAdd={(sub) => {
                updateClass(cls, prev => ({
                  ...prev,
                  subjects: [...prev.subjects, sub]
                }));
              }}
            />

          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ================= SUBJECT UI ================= */
function SubjectUI({ stored, teachers, onAdd }) {

  const [subId, setSubId] = useState("");
  const [teacherId, setTeacherId] = useState("");
  const [type, setType] = useState("compulsory");

  const available = GLOBAL_SUBJECTS.filter(
    s => !stored.some(x => x.code === s.code)
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <select onChange={e => setSubId(e.target.value)}>
        <option>Select Subject</option>
        {available.map(s => (
          <option key={s._id} value={s._id}>{s.name}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <select onChange={e => setTeacherId(e.target.value)}>
        <option>Select Teacher</option>
        {teachers.map(t => (
          <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <select onChange={e => setType(e.target.value)}>
        {subjectTypes.map(t => (
          <option key={t}>{t}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <button onClick={() => {
        if (!subId || !teacherId) return;

        const sub = GLOBAL_SUBJECTS.find(s => s._id === subId);
        const teacher = teachers.find(t => t.id === teacherId);

        onAdd({
          subjectId: sub._id,
          name: sub.name,
          code: sub.code,
          type,
          teacherId: teacher.id,
          teacherName: teacher.name
        });
      }}>
        Add
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default SchoolSetup;

.row {

// export default AdminDashboard;


import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../../../context/authContext";
import StatsCard from "../../component/stats/stats/statsCard";
// import JoinRequests from "../../component/joinRequests/joinRequests";
import styles from "./dashboard.module.css";

import { getUsers } from "../../../../api/userAPI";
import { getCourses } from "../../../../api/courseApi";
import { getSubjects } from "../../../../api/subjectApi";

function AdminDashboard() {

  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [students, setStudents] = useState([]);
  const [teachers, setTeachers] = useState([]);
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([]);
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  /* ================= FETCH REAL DATA ================= */

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAll();
  }, []);

  const fetchAll = async () => {
    try {
      const users = await getUsers();
      const courseRes = await getCourses();
      const subjectRes = await getSubjects();

      setStudents(users.filter(u => u.role === "student"));
      setTeachers(users.filter(u => u.role === "teacher"));

      setCourses(courseRes || []);
      setSubjects(subjectRes || []);

    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Dashboard fetch error:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  /* ================= CALCULATIONS ================= */

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      totalStudents: students.length,
      totalTeachers: teachers.length,
      totalCourses: courses.length,
      totalSubjects: subjects.length,

    };
  }, [students, teachers, courses, subjects]);

  if (loading) return <p>Loading...</p>;

  return (
    <div className={styles.dashboard}>

      {/* HEADER */}
      <div className={styles.header}>
        <div>
          <h1 className={styles.greeting}>
            Welcome, {user?.name?.split(" ")[0] || "Admin"}
          </h1>
          <p className={styles.subtitle}>
            Institute Dashboard Overview
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ================= STATS ================= */}
      <div className={styles.statsGrid}>

        <StatsCard
          title="Total Students"
          value={stats.totalStudents.toString()}
          color="blue"
        />

        <StatsCard
          title="Total Teachers"
          value={stats.totalTeachers.toString()}
          color="green"
        />

        <StatsCard
          title="Courses"
          value={stats.totalCourses.toString()}
          color="purple"
        />

        <StatsCard
          title="Subjects"
          value={stats.totalSubjects.toString()}
          color="orange"
        />

      </div>

      {/* ================= SIMPLE LIST ================= */}
      <div className={styles.mainGrid}>

        {/* STUDENTS */}
        <div className={styles.card}>
          <h3>Students</h3>

          {students.slice(0, 5).map(s => (
            <div key={s._id} className={styles.studentItem}>
              <span>{s.name}</span>
              <small>
                {s.courseId?.name || "No Course"} | Sem {s.semester || "-"}
              </small>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* TEACHERS */}
        <div className={styles.card}>
          <h3>Teachers</h3>

          {teachers.slice(0, 5).map(t => (
            <div key={t._id} className={styles.studentItem}>
              <span>{t.name}</span>
              <small>
                {t.departmentId?.name || "No Dept"}
              </small>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* COURSES */}
        <div className={styles.card}>
          <h3>Courses</h3>

          {courses.slice(0, 5).map(c => (
            <div key={c._id} className={styles.studentItem}>
              <span>{c.name}</span>
            </div>
          ))}





        </div>

      </div>

      {/* ================= JOIN REQUESTS ================= */}
      {/* <div className={styles.bottomGrid}>
        <JoinRequests requests={joinRequests} />
      </div> */}

    </div>
  );
}

export default AdminDashboard;

import styles from './academicDetails.module.css';

function AcademicDetails({ student }) {
  if (!student) return <p className={styles.empty}>No Data</p>;

  return (
    <div className={styles.card}>
      <h3>Academic Details</h3>

      <div className={styles.row}>
        <span>Branch:</span>
        <span>{student.branch}</span>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.row}>
        <span>Year:</span>
        <span>{student.year}</span>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.row}>
        <span>GPA:</span>
        <span>{student.gpa}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default AcademicDetails;

📁 component\createAssignments\assignment.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../../../context/authContext";
import styles from "./assignment.module.css";

import {
  getAssignments,
  createAssignment
} from "../../../../api/assignmentApi";

import {
  uploadAssignmentFile
} from "../../../../api/uploadApi";

import { getMyInstitute } from "../../../../api/instituteApi";

export default function Assignment() {

  const { currentUser } = useAuth();

  const isTeacher = ["teacher", "hod", "admin"].includes(currentUser?.role);

  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState([]);
  const [config, setConfig] = useState(null);

  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    subject: "",
    title: "",
    description: "",
    label: "normal",
    deadline: "",
    instructions: "",
  });

  /* ================= LOAD ================= */
  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, []);

  const load = async () => {
    const a = await getAssignments();
    const inst = await getMyInstitute();

    setAssignments(a);
    setConfig(inst?.config);
  };

  /* ================= SUBJECT FILTER ================= */

  const teacherSubjects = useMemo(() => {
    if (!config || !currentUser) return [];

    const list = [];

    /* SCHOOL */
    config.classes?.forEach(cls => {
      cls.subjects?.forEach(s => {
        if (s.teacher?.toString() === currentUser._id.toString()) {
          list.push({
            ...s.subject,
            classId: cls.class,
          });
        }
      });
    });

    /* COLLEGE */
    config.departments?.forEach(d => {
      d.courses?.forEach(c => {
        c.structure?.forEach(sem => {
          sem.subjects?.forEach(s => {
            if (s.teacher?.toString() === currentUser._id.toString()) {
              list.push({
                ...s.subject,
                department: d.department,
                course: c.course,
                semester: sem.number,
              });
            }
          });
        });
      });
    });

    return list;
  }, [config, currentUser]);

  /* ================= DRAG DROP ================= */

  const handleDrop = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
  };

  /* ================= CREATE ================= */

  const handleCreate = async () => {
    try {
      if (!form.title || !form.subject) {
        return alert("Fill required fields");
      }

      let fileData = {};

      if (file) {
        const res = await uploadAssignmentFile(file, setProgress);
        fileData = res;
      }

      await createAssignment({
        ...form,
        ...fileData,
      });

      alert("Assignment Created");

      setFile(null);
      setProgress(0);
      setForm({
        subject: "",
        title: "",
        description: "",
        label: "normal",
        deadline: "",
        instructions: "",
      });

      load();

    } catch (err) {
      alert(err.response?.data?.msg || "Error");
    }
  };

  /* ================= UI ================= */

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>

      {/* ================= CREATE ================= */}
      {isTeacher && (
        <>
          <h2>Create Assignment</h2>

          <div className={styles.form}>

            {/* SUBJECT FILTERED */}
            <select
              value={form.subject}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({ ...form, subject: e.target.value })
              }
            >
              <option value="">Select Subject</option>
              {teacherSubjects.map((s) => (
                <option key={s._id} value={s._id}>
                  {s.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <input
              placeholder="Title"
              value={form.title}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({ ...form, title: e.target.value })
              }
            />

            <textarea
              placeholder="Question"
              value={form.description}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })
              }
            />

            {/* DRAG DROP */}
            <div
              className={styles.drop}
              onDrop={handleDrop}
              onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
            >
              {file ? file.name : "Drag & Drop or Click"}
              <input
                type="file"
                onChange={(e) => setFile(e.target.files[0])}
              />
            </div>

            {/* PROGRESS */}
            {progress > 0 && (
              <div className={styles.progress}>
                <div
                  style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
                  className={styles.bar}
                />
              </div>
            )}

            <select
              value={form.label}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({ ...form, label: e.target.value })
              }
            >
              <option value="normal">Normal</option>
              <option value="important">Important</option>
              <option value="compulsory">Compulsory</option>
            </select>

            <input
              type="date"
              value={form.deadline}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({ ...form, deadline: e.target.value })
              }
            />

            <textarea
              placeholder="Instructions"
              value={form.instructions}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({ ...form, instructions: e.target.value })
              }
            />

            <button onClick={handleCreate}>
              Create Assignment
            </button>

          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {/* ================= LIST ================= */}
      <h2>Assignments</h2>

      {assignments.map((a) => (
        <div key={a._id} className={styles.card}>

          <div className={styles.rowTop}>
            <h3>{a.title}</h3>
            <span className={`${styles.badge} ${styles[a.label]}`}>
              {a.label}
            </span>
          </div>

          <p>{a.description}</p>

          <div className={styles.meta}>
            <span>{a.subject?.name}</span>
            <span>
              Deadline: {new Date(a.deadline).toLocaleDateString()}
            </span>
          </div>

        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Outlet } from 'react-router-dom'; // ✅ ADD THIS
import Sidebar from './../dSidebar/dSidebar';
import Navbar from './../navbar/navbar';
import styles from './Dashboardlayout.module.css';

function DashboardLayout() {
  const [sidebarCollapsed, setSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className={styles.layout}>
      <Sidebar 
        collapsed={sidebarCollapsed} 
        onToggle={() => setSidebarCollapsed(!sidebarCollapsed)} 
      />

      <div className={`${styles.mainContent} ${sidebarCollapsed ? styles.expanded : ''}`}>
        <Navbar onMenuToggle={() => setSidebarCollapsed(!sidebarCollapsed)} />

        <main className={styles.content}>
          <Outlet /> {/* ✅ THIS FIXES EVERYTHING */}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default DashboardLayout;

📁 component\dataView\DataView.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../../../../context/authContext";
import styles from "./DataView.module.css";

import { getUsers } from "../../../../api/userAPI";

function DataView({ type = "students", instituteType = "college" }) {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  const [department, setDepartment] = useState("");
  const [semester, setSemester] = useState("");
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState("");

  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState("name");
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState("asc");

  const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 20;

  /* ================= FETCH ================= */
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchData = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);

        const res = await getUsers({
          role:
            type === "students"
              ? "student"
              : type === "teachers"
              ? "teacher"
              : undefined,

          instituteId: user?.instituteId, // 🔥 MAIN FIX

          departmentId:
            instituteType === "college" ? department : undefined,

          semester:
            instituteType === "college" ? semester : undefined,
        });

        setUsers(res || []);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Data fetch error:", err);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    if (user?.instituteId) {
      fetchData();
    }
  }, [type, department, semester, instituteType, user]);

  /* ================= RESET FILTERS ================= */
  useEffect(() => {
    setDepartment("");
    setSemester("");
    setPage(1);
  }, [instituteType]);

  /* ================= DATA SELECT ================= */
  let data = [];

  if (type === "students") {
    data = users.filter((u) => u.role === "student");
  }

  if (type === "teachers") {
    data = users.filter((u) => u.role === "teacher");
  }

  if (type === "admins") {
    data = users.filter(
      (u) => u.role === "admin" || u.role === "hod"
    );
  }

  /* ================= RBAC + ROLE FILTER ================= */
  const roleFiltered = data.filter((item) => {
    if (roleFilter && item.role !== roleFilter) return false;

    if (user.role === "superadmin") return true;

    if (user.role === "admin") {
      return (
        item.instituteId?._id === user.instituteId ||
        item.instituteId === user.instituteId
      );
    }

    if (user.role === "hod") {
      return (
        item.departmentId?._id === user.departmentId ||
        item.departmentId === user.departmentId
      );
    }

    return true;
  });

  /* ================= SEARCH ================= */
  const searched = roleFiltered.filter((item) =>
    (item.name || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  /* ================= SORT ================= */
  const sorted = useMemo(() => {
    return [...searched].sort((a, b) => {
      const valA = a[sortKey] || "";
      const valB = b[sortKey] || "";

      if (sortOrder === "asc") return valA > valB ? 1 : -1;
      return valA < valB ? 1 : -1;
    });
  }, [searched, sortKey, sortOrder]);

  /* ================= PAGINATION ================= */
  const start = (page - 1) * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
  const paginated = sorted.slice(start, start + ITEMS_PER_PAGE);
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(sorted.length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE);

  /* ================= DEPARTMENTS ================= */
  const departments = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map();

    users.forEach((u) => {
      if (u.departmentId) {
        map.set(u.departmentId._id, u.departmentId);
      }
    });

    return Array.from(map.values());
  }, [users]);

  /* ================= COLUMNS ================= */
  const columnsMap = {
    students: ["name", "rollNumber", "semester"],
    teachers: ["name", "employeeId", "departmentId"],
    admins: ["name", "email", "role"],
  };

  const columns = columnsMap[type] || [];

  if (loading) return <div>Loading...</div>;

  /* ================= UI ================= */
  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <div className={styles.header}>
        <h1 className={styles.title}>{type.toUpperCase()}</h1>

        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search..."
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className={styles.searchInput}
        />
      </div>

      {/* ================= FILTERS ================= */}
      <div className={styles.filters}>

        {/* 🔥 COLLEGE ONLY */}
        {instituteType === "college" && (
          <>
            <select
              value={department}
              onChange={(e) => setDepartment(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="">Department</option>
              {departments.map((dep) => (
                <option key={dep._id} value={dep._id}>
                  {dep.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={semester}
              onChange={(e) => setSemester(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="">Semester</option>
              {[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8].map((s) => (
                <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </>
        )}

        {/* 🔥 ROLE FILTER */}
        <select
          value={roleFilter}
          onChange={(e) => setRoleFilter(e.target.value)}
        >
          <option value="">Role</option>
          <option value="student">Student</option>
          <option value="teacher">Teacher</option>
        </select>

        {/* SORT */}
        <select
          value={sortKey}
          onChange={(e) => setSortKey(e.target.value)}
        >
          {columns.map((col) => (
            <option key={col} value={col}>{col}</option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <select
          value={sortOrder}
          onChange={(e) => setSortOrder(e.target.value)}
        >
          <option value="asc">ASC</option>
          <option value="desc">DESC</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* ================= TABLE ================= */}
      <div className={styles.tableCard}>
        <table className={styles.table}>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              {columns.map((col) => (
                <th key={col}>{col}</th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            {paginated.map((item) => (
              <tr key={item._id}>
                {columns.map((col) => (
                  <td key={col}>
                    {col === "name" ? (
                      <span
                        onClick={() =>
                          navigate(`/${user.role}/${type}/${item._id}`)
                        }
                        style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}
                      >
                        {item.name}
                      </span>
                    ) : col === "departmentId" ? (
                      item.departmentId?.name || "-"
                    ) : (
                      item[col] || "-"
                    )}
                  </td>
                ))}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      {/* ================= PAGINATION ================= */}
      <div className={styles.pagination}>
        <button disabled={page === 1} onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}>
          Prev
        </button>

        <span>
          Page {page} / {totalPages || 1}
        </span>

        <button
          disabled={page === totalPages}
          onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}
        >
          Next
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default DataView;

📁 component\Details\Details.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useData } from "../../context/dataContext";
import styles from "./Details.module.css";

function DetailsPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { students, teachers, colleges } = useData();

  const allData = [...students, ...teachers, ...colleges];
  const item = allData.find((d) => d._id === id);

  if (!item) return <div className={styles.notFound}>Not Found</div>;

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <div className={styles.card}>

        {/* HEADER */}
        <div className={styles.header}>
          <div className={styles.avatar}>
            {(item.name || item.collegeName)?.charAt(0)}
          </div>

          <div>
            <h1 className={styles.title}>
              {item.name || item.collegeName}
            </h1>
            <p className={styles.subtitle}>
              {item.email || item.code || "Details"}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* DATA GRID */}
        <div className={styles.grid}>
          {Object.entries(item).map(([key, value]) => (
            <div key={key} className={styles.field}>
              <span className={styles.label}>{key}</span>
              <span className={styles.value}>
                {typeof value === "object"
                  ? JSON.stringify(value)
                  : value?.toString()}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default DetailsPage;

/*COMPONENT*/
function Sidebar({ collapsed, onToggle }) {

  const { user, logout } = useAuth(); // ✅ FIXED
  const location = useLocation();

  /*ROLE*/
  const role = user?.role?.toLowerCase() || "student";

  const menuItems = roleConfig[role]?.sidebar || [];

  /*PROFILE */
  const profilePath = `/${role}/profile`;

  /*ICON HELPER*/
  const getIcon = (iconName) => {
    const IconComponent = Icons[iconName];
    return IconComponent ? <IconComponent /> : null;
  };

  return (
    <aside className={`${styles.sidebar} ${collapsed ? styles.collapsed : ""}`}>

      {/*LOGO */}
      <div className={styles.logoSection}>
        <div className={styles.logoDiv}>
          <img src="/logo.png" alt="Logo" className={styles.logo} />
          {!collapsed && <h1>uniCore</h1>}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* NAV*/}
      <nav className={styles.nav}>
        <ul className={styles.menu}>

          <li>
            <button
              className={`${styles.menuItem} ${styles.toggleBtn}`}
              onClick={onToggle}
              title={collapsed ? "Toggle Sidebar" : ""}
            >
              <span className={styles.icon}>
                <svg >
                  <path
                    d={
                      collapsed
                        ? "M10 6L8.59 7.41 13.17 12l-4.58 4.59L10 18l6-6z"
                        : "M15.41 7.41L14 6l-6 6 6 6 1.41-1.41L10.83 12z"
                    }
                  />
                </svg>
              </span>
              {!collapsed && (
                <span className={styles.label}></span>
              )}
            </button>
          </li>


          {/* Dynamic Menu */}
          {menuItems.map((item, index) => {
            const isActive = location.pathname === item.path;

            return (
              <li key={index}>
                <Link
                  to={item.path}
                  className={`${styles.menuItem} ${isActive ? styles.active : ""}`}
                  title={collapsed ? item.name : ""}
                >
                  <span className={styles.icon}>
                    {getIcon(item.icon)}
                  </span>

                  {!collapsed && (
                    <span className={styles.label}>{item.name}</span>
                  )}

                  {isActive && <span className={styles.activeIndicator} />}
                </Link>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      </nav>

      {/* USER */}
      <div className={styles.userSection}>
        <Link to={profilePath} className={styles.userInfo}>
          <div className={styles.avatar}>
            {user?.name?.charAt(0) || "U"}
          </div>

          {!collapsed && (
            <div className={styles.userDetails}>
              <span className={styles.userName}>
                {user?.name || "User"}
              </span>

              <span className={styles.userRole}>
                {roleConfig[role]?.label || role}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </Link>

        <button
          className={styles.logoutBtn}
          onClick={logout}
          title="Logout"
        >
          <span className={styles.icon}>
            {getIcon("logout")}
          </span>

          {!collapsed && <span>Logout</span>}
        </button>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

export default Sidebar;

📁 component\grrades\grades.jsx

import { useData } from "../../context/dataContext";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";
import styles from "./grades.module.css";

export default function Grades() {
  const { getStudentGrades } = useData(); // ✅ correct
  const { currentUser } = useAuth();

  // ✅ directly get grades
  const studentGrades = getStudentGrades(currentUser?.id) || [];

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <h2 className={styles.heading}>My Grades</h2>

      {studentGrades.length === 0 ? (
        <p className={styles.noData}>No grades available</p>
      ) : (
        <table className={styles.table}>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Subject</th>
              <th>Assignment</th>
              <th>Marks</th>
              <th>Grade</th>
              <th>Feedback</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            {studentGrades.map((grade) => (
              <tr key={grade.id}>
                <td>{grade.subject}</td>
                <td>{grade.title}</td>
                <td>{grade.marks}</td>
                <td className={getGradeClass(grade.grade)}>
                  {grade.grade}
                </td>
                <td>{grade.feedback || "—"}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// helper for coloring grade
function getGradeClass(grade) {
  switch (grade) {
    case "A":
      return styles.a;
    case "B":
      return styles.b;
    case "C":
      return styles.c;
    case "D":
      return styles.d;
    default:
      return styles.f;
  }
}

📁 component\InstituteDetails\InstituteDetails.jsx

import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";
import { useData } from "../../context/dataContext";
import styles from "./InstituteDetails.module.css";

function InstituteDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { currentUser } = useAuth();
  const { institutes, users } = useData();

  // 🔥 RBAC FILTER
  const filteredInstitutes =
    currentUser.role === "superadmin"
      ? institutes
      : institutes.filter((inst) => inst._id === currentUser.instituteId);

  const institute = filteredInstitutes.find((i) => i._id === id);

  if (!institute) return <div className={styles.empty}>Not Found</div>;

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      
      {/* 🔥 HEADER */}
      <div className={styles.header}>
        <img src={institute.media.logo} className={styles.logo} />

        <div>
          <h1>{institute.name}</h1>
          <p className={styles.code}>{institute.code}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 🔥 BUILDING IMAGE */}
      <div className={styles.banner}>
        <img src={institute.media.building} alt="building" />
      </div>

      {/* 🔥 ABOUT */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h2>About</h2>
        <p>{institute.about}</p>
      </div>

      {/* 🔥 GALLERY */}
      {institute.media.gallery?.length > 0 && (
        <div className={styles.card}>
          <h2>Gallery</h2>
          <div className={styles.gallery}>
            {institute.media.gallery.map((img, i) => (
              <img key={i} src={img} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 🔥 STRUCTURE */}
      <div className={styles.card}>
        <h2>Structure</h2>

        {/* COLLEGE */}
        {institute.type === "college" &&
          Object.entries(institute.structure).map(([dept, data]) => (
            <div key={dept} className={styles.block}>
              <h3>{dept}</h3>

              <p className={styles.sub}>
                HOD:{" "}
                {
                  users.find((u) => u._id === data.hodId)?.name ||
                  "Not Assigned"
                }
              </p>

              {Object.entries(data.years || {}).map(([year, yData]) => (
                <div key={year} className={styles.innerBlock}>
                  <h4>Year {year}</h4>

                  {Object.entries(yData.semesters || {}).map(
                    ([sem, semData]) => (
                      <div key={sem} className={styles.sem}>
                        <span>Sem {sem}:</span>
                        <div className={styles.tags}>
                          {semData.subjects.map((sub) => (
                            <span key={sub}>{sub}</span>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ))}

        {/* SCHOOL */}
        {institute.type === "school" &&
          Object.entries(institute.structure).map(([cls, data]) => (
            <div key={cls} className={styles.block}>
              <h3>Class {cls}</h3>

              <p className={styles.sub}>
                Sections: {data.sections.join(", ")}
              </p>

              {data.streams?.map((stream) => (
                <div key={stream} className={styles.innerBlock}>
                  <h4>{stream}</h4>

                  <div className={styles.tags}>
                    {data.subjects[stream].map((sub) => (
                      <span key={sub}>{sub}</span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default InstituteDetail;

📁 component\joinRequests\joinRequests.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import styles from './joinRequests.module.css';

function JoinRequests({ requests, filter = 'pending' }) {
  const { approveRequest, rejectRequest } = useAuth();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(filter);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(null);

  const filteredRequests = requests.filter(r => r.status === activeTab);

  const handleApprove = async (id) => {
    setProcessing(id);
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500)); // Simulate async
    approveRequest(id);
    setProcessing(null);
  };

  const handleReject = async (id) => {
    setProcessing(id);
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
    rejectRequest(id);
    setProcessing(null);
  };

  const formatTime = (date) => {
    const diff = Date.now() - new Date(date).getTime();
    const minutes = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
    if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;
    const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
    if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;
    return `${Math.floor(hours / 24)}d ago`;
  };

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <div className={styles.header}>
        <h3 className={styles.title}>Join Requests</h3>
        <div className={styles.tabs}>
          {['pending', 'approved', 'rejected'].map(tab => (
            <button
              key={tab}
              className={`${styles.tab} ${activeTab === tab ? styles.activeTab : ''}`}
              onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab)}
            >
              {tab.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + tab.slice(1)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.list}>
        {filteredRequests.length === 0 ? (
          <div className={styles.empty}>No {activeTab} requests</div>
        ) : (
          filteredRequests.map(request => (
            <div key={request._id} className={styles.requestItem}>
              <div className={styles.avatar}>
                {request.name.charAt(0)}
              </div>
              <div className={styles.info}>
                <span className={styles.name}>{request.name}</span>
                <span className={styles.details}>
                  {request.role === 'student' 
                    ? `${request.class} • ${request.branch}`
                    : `${request.department} • Teacher`
                  }
                </span>
                <span className={styles.time}>{formatTime(request.requestedAt)}</span>
              </div>
              {activeTab === 'pending' && (
                <div className={styles.actions}>
                  <button
                    className={styles.approveBtn}
                    onClick={() => handleApprove(request._id)}
                    disabled={processing === request._id}
                  >
                    {processing === request._id ? '...' : 'Accept'}
                  </button>
                  <button
                    className={styles.rejectBtn}
                    onClick={() => handleReject(request._id)}
                    disabled={processing === request._id}
                  >
                    Reject
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
              {activeTab !== 'pending' && (
                <span className={`${styles.statusBadge} ${styles[activeTab]}`}>
                  {activeTab}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      <button className={styles.viewAllBtn}>View Full Reports</button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default JoinRequests;

📁 component\markAttandence\Attendance.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../../../context/authContext";
import styles from "./attendance.module.css";

import { getUsers } from "../../../../api/userAPI";
import { getSubjects } from "../../../../api/subjectApi";
import { getMyInstitute } from "../../../../api/instituteApi";
import { markAttendance } from "../../../../api/attandenceApi";

export default function Attendance() {

  const { user } = useAuth();

  /* ================= BLOCK ACCESS ================= */
  if (user?.role !== "teacher") {
    return (
      <div className={styles.center}>
        Only assigned teachers can mark attendance
      </div>
    );
  }

  const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];

  /* ================= STATE ================= */
  const [students, setStudents] = useState([]);
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState([]);
  const [config, setConfig] = useState(null);

  const [subject, setSubject] = useState("");
  const [allowedStudents, setAllowedStudents] = useState([]);

  const [attendanceState, setAttendanceState] = useState({});

  /* ================= FETCH ================= */
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetch = async () => {
      const users = await getUsers();
      const subs = await getSubjects();
      const inst = await getMyInstitute();

      setStudents(users.filter(u => u.role === "student"));
      setSubjects(subs);
      setConfig(inst?.config);
    };

    fetch();
  }, []);

  /* ================= FILTER SUBJECTS ================= */
  const teacherSubjects = [];

  if (config) {
    /* SCHOOL */
    config.classes?.forEach(cls => {
      cls.subjects?.forEach(s => {
        if (s.teacher === user._id) {
          teacherSubjects.push({
            ...s.subject,
            classId: cls.class,
            section: cls.section || "A"
          });
        }
      });
    });

    /* COLLEGE */
    config.departments?.forEach(d => {
      d.courses?.forEach(c => {
        c.structure?.forEach(sem => {
          sem.subjects?.forEach(s => {
            if (s.teacher === user._id) {
              teacherSubjects.push({
                ...s.subject,
                department: d.department,
                course: c.course,
                semester: sem.number
              });
            }
          });
        });
      });
    });
  }

  /* ================= SELECT SUBJECT ================= */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!subject) return;

    const selected = teacherSubjects.find(s => s._id === subject);

    if (!selected) return;

    /* 🔥 FILTER STUDENTS BASED ON SUBJECT */
    let filtered = students;

    if (selected.classId) {
      filtered = students.filter(s => s.class === selected.classId);
    } else {
      filtered = students.filter(
        s =>
          s.departmentId?._id === selected.department &&
          s.courseId?._id === selected.course &&
          s.semester === selected.semester
      );
    }

    setAllowedStudents(filtered);

  }, [subject, students]);

  /* ================= TOGGLE ================= */
  const toggle = (id) => {
    setAttendanceState(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [id]: !prev[id]
    }));
  };

  /* ================= SUBMIT ================= */
  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    try {
      const selected = teacherSubjects.find(s => s._id === subject);

      const payload = {
        subject,
        students: allowedStudents.map(s => ({
          student: s._id,
          status: attendanceState[s._id] ? "present" : "absent"
        }))
      };

      if (selected.classId) {
        payload.classId = selected.classId;
        payload.section = selected.section;
      } else {
        payload.department = selected.department;
        payload.course = selected.course;
        payload.semester = selected.semester;
      }

      await markAttendance(payload);

      alert("Attendance saved");

    } catch (err) {
      alert(err.response?.data?.msg || "Error");
    }
  };

  /* ================= UI ================= */
  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>

      <div className={styles.header}>
        <h2>Attendance</h2>
        <span>{today}</span>
      </div>

      {/* SUBJECT SELECT */}
      <select onChange={e => setSubject(e.target.value)}>
        <option value="">Select Subject</option>
        {teacherSubjects.map(s => (
          <option key={s._id} value={s._id}>
            {s.name}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>

      {/* STUDENTS */}
      <div className={styles.table}>
        {allowedStudents.map(s => (
          <div key={s._id} className={styles.row}>
            <span>{s.rollNumber}</span>
            <span>{s.name}</span>

            <div
              className={`${styles.box} ${
                attendanceState[s._id] ? styles.present : ""
              }`}
              onClick={() => toggle(s._id)}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {subject && allowedStudents.length > 0 && (
        <button className={styles.submit} onClick={handleSubmit}>
          Save Attendance
        </button>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

.container {
  padding: 20px;
  color: white;

import { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../../../context/authContext"; // ✅ correct path
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import styles from "./navbar.module.css";

function Navbar({ onMenuToggle }) {
  const { user } = useAuth(); // ✅ FIXED (was currentUser)

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [showNotifications, setShowNotifications] = useState(false);

  const notifications = [
    {
      id: 1,
      title: "New Assignment",
      message: "DSA assignment due tomorrow",
      time: "5m ago",
      unread: true,
    },
    {
      id: 2,
      title: "Attendance Marked",
      message: "Your attendance has been marked for OS",
      time: "1h ago",
      unread: true,
    },
    {
      id: 3,
      title: "Grade Updated",
      message: "DBMS mid-sem marks uploaded",
      time: "2h ago",
      unread: false,
    },
  ];

  /* ================= ROLE ================= */
  const role = user?.role?.toLowerCase();

  /* ================= PROFILE PATH ================= */
  const profilePath = role ? `/${role}/profile` : "/login";

  return (
    <header className={styles.navbar}>
      {/* ================= LEFT ================= */}
      <div className={styles.left}>
        <div className={styles.searchBox}>
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" className={styles.searchIcon}>
            <path d="M15.5 14h-.79l-.28-.27C15.41 12.59 16 11.11 16 9.5 16 5.91 13.09 3 9.5 3S3 5.91 3 9.5 5.91 16 9.5 16c1.61 0 3.09-.59 4.23-1.57l.27.28v.79l5 4.99L20.49 19l-4.99-5zm-6 0C7.01 14 5 11.99 5 9.5S7.01 5 9.5 5 14 7.01 14 9.5 11.99 14 9.5 14z" />
          </svg>

          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className={styles.searchInput}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ================= RIGHT ================= */}
      <div className={styles.right}>
        {/* 🔔 Notifications */}
        <div className={styles.notificationWrapper}>
          <button
            className={styles.iconBtn}
            onClick={() => setShowNotifications(!showNotifications)}
          >
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path d="M12 22c1.1 0 2-.9 2-2h-4c0 1.1.89 2 2 2zm6-6v-5c0-3.07-1.64-5.64-4.5-6.32V4c0-.83-.67-1.5-1.5-1.5s-1.5.67-1.5 1.5v.68C7.63 5.36 6 7.92 6 11v5l-2 2v1h16v-1l-2-2z" />
            </svg>

            <span className={styles.badge}>
              {notifications.filter((n) => n.unread).length}
            </span>
          </button>

          {showNotifications && (
            <div className={styles.notificationDropdown}>
              <div className={styles.dropdownHeader}>
                <h4>Notifications</h4>
                <button className={styles.markAllRead}>
                  Mark all read
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className={styles.notificationList}>
                {notifications.map((notif) => (
                  <div
                    key={notif.id}
                    className={`${styles.notificationItem} ${
                      notif.unread ? styles.unread : ""
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className={styles.notificationContent}>
                      <span className={styles.notificationTitle}>
                        {notif.title}
                      </span>
                      <span className={styles.notificationMessage}>
                        {notif.message}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <span className={styles.notificationTime}>
                      {notif.time}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              <button className={styles.viewAll}>
                View All Notifications
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* 💬 Messages */}
        <button className={styles.iconBtn}>
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path d="M20 2H4c-1.1 0-1.99.9-1.99 2L2 22l4-4h14c1.1 0 2-.9 2-2V4c0-1.1-.9-2-2-2zm-2 12H6v-2h12v2zm0-3H6V9h12v2zm0-3H6V6h12v2z" />
          </svg>
        </button>

        {/* 👤 PROFILE AVATAR */}
        <Link to={profilePath}>
          <div className={styles.userAvatar}>
            {user?.avatar ? (
              <img
                src={user.avatar}
                alt="profile"
                style={{
                  width: "100%",
                  height: "100%",
                  borderRadius: "12px",
                  objectFit: "cover",
                }}
              />
            ) : (
              user?.name?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || "U"
            )}
          </div>
        </Link>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

export default Navbar;

📁 component\profile\profile.jsx
--------------------------------
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "./../../../../context/authContext";
import styles from "./profile.module.css";

function Profile() {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    name: user?.name || "",
    bio: user?.bio || "",
    avatar: user?.avatar || "",
  });

  if (!user) return <div>Loading...</div>;

  const role = user.role?.toLowerCase();

  /* ================= HANDLE CHANGE ================= */
  const handleChange = (e) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;

    setFormData((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  };

  /* ================= HANDLE SAVE ================= */
  const handleSave = async () => {
    try {
      // 🔥 call your backend API (you must create this)
      // await updateProfileAPI(formData);

      console.log("Updated Data:", formData);

      setIsEditing(false);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Profile update failed", err);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      {/* ================= HEADER ================= */}
      <div className={styles.header}>
        <div className={styles.avatarWrapper}>
          <img
            src={
              formData.avatar ||
              `https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=${formData.name}`
            }
            alt="profile"
            className={styles.avatar}
          />

          {isEditing && (
            <input
              type="text"
              name="avatar"
              placeholder="Avatar URL"
              value={formData.avatar}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className={styles.input}
            />
          )}
        </div>

        <div className={styles.info}>
          {isEditing ? (
            <input
              type="text"
              name="name"
              value={formData.name}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className={styles.input}
            />
          ) : (
            <h2>{user.name}</h2>
          )}

          <span className={styles.role}>{role.toUpperCase()}</span>
        </div>

        <button
          className={styles.editBtn}
          onClick={() =>
            isEditing ? handleSave() : setIsEditing(true)
          }
        >
          {isEditing ? "Save" : "Edit"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ================= ABOUT ================= */}
      <div className={styles.section}>
        <h3>About</h3>

        {isEditing ? (
          <textarea
            name="bio"
            value={formData.bio}
            onChange={handleChange}
            className={styles.textarea}
          />
        ) : (
          <p>{user.bio || "No bio added"}</p>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ================= DETAILS ================= */}
      <div className={styles.section}>
        <h3>Details</h3>

        <div className={styles.grid}>
          <div>
            <label>Email</label>
            <span>{user.email}</span>
          </div>

          {/* ================= STUDENT ================= */}
          {role === "student" && (
            <>
              <div>
                <label>Roll No</label>
                <span>{user.rollNumber || "-"}</span>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label>Course</label>
                <span>{user.courseId?.name || "-"}</span>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label>Semester</label>
                <span>{user.semester || "-"}</span>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label>Class</label>
                <span>{user.classId?.name || "-"}</span>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label>Section</label>
                <span>{user.section || "-"}</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {/* ================= TEACHER ================= */}
          {role === "teacher" && (
            <>
              <div>
                <label>Employee ID</label>
                <span>{user.employeeId || "-"}</span>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label>Subjects</label>
                <span>
                  {user.subjects?.length
                    ? user.subjects.map((s) => s.name).join(", ")
                    : "-"}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label>Department</label>
                <span>{user.departmentId?.name || "-"}</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {/* ================= HOD / ADMIN ================= */}
          {(role === "admin" || role === "hod") && (
            <>
              <div>
                <label>Employee ID</label>
                <span>{user.employeeId || "-"}</span>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label>Department</label>
                <span>{user.departmentId?.name || "-"}</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Profile;

import styles from './recentActivity.module.css';

function RecentActivity({ activities = [] }) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <h3>Recent Activity</h3>

      {activities.length === 0 ? (
        <p className={styles.empty}>No Activity</p>
      ) : (
        activities.map((act, index) => (
          <div key={index} className={styles.item}>
            <p>{act.action}</p>
            <span>{act.time}</span>
          </div>
        ))
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default RecentActivity;
